## Haiti added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Haiti's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **28 files, every one of them teacher-side**: the nine *Programme à Compétences Minimales* (PCM) volumes for Années Fondamentales 1–9 (MENFP, Direction de l'Enseignement Fondamental, November 2019), the three IPN *Programme détaillé, Matières de base* volumes (4e, 5e, 6e année), the three *Programme Pédagogique Opérationnel* (PPO) Mathématiques fascicules for the 3e cycle (7e, 8e, 9e année, 1989-1990), the eight Nectar (MENFP) teacher sequences (four for 7e année, four for 8e année), the Nouveau Secondaire *Programme-Cadre* and the four NS detailed programmes (NS1–NS4) — every one of the 28 carrying a full Mathématiques section — and placed **1,639 items, every one of them TEACHER/CURRICULUM EVIDENCE**: **Haiti has no pupil Maths textbook in hand under any label**, so Haiti is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure and is named in the countries line and in the rows below only, on the standing evidence-only precedent (Ghana / Bahamas / Canada / Myanmar). The expansion map's count for Haiti is wrong: it carries 15, counting only the books.json entries whose subject field reads “Mathematics”, and the download list claims 43 teacher files; the true maths-bearing set is 28 files — the nine “All subjects” PCM volumes, the three IPN detailed programmes and the NS Programme-Cadre all carry full maths sections the count missed (a record-side fault, reported, not fixed by this merge); textbooks.tsv is likewise a stale 28-row index naming only a subset of the secured files. Haiti's official pupil books (the MENFP/FAGEM *Manuels uniques* / “Mon manuel” series) are in hand for other subjects only, never for Mathematics, and the two remanié programme leads (a 7e année fascicule and a 4e année detailed programme) are not in hand — and programme documents are not textbooks under the 2026-11-10 textbook-test ruling in any case — so **Haiti stays PARTIAL for Maths, teacher evidence only**. All 28 files were read page-by-page from the secured PDFs in phase3-staging, byte-exact against their books.json records; nothing was downloaded. Read depth: the PPO 8e fascicule and the IPN 4e and 6e programmes are image-only scans and were read by OCR in full, spot-checked against the page images — where OCR garbled a worked example's digits, the placement teaches the corrected arithmetic and the case is flagged; PPO 7e PDF p. 48 and PPO 9e PDF pp. 20, 30, 31 and 32 were verified blank, separator or title pages carrying no objectives. Printed-page bases: PCM printed page = PDF page − 1; IPN 4e and 6e printed page = PDF page − 9, IPN 5e printed folios equal PDF pages; PPO 7e printed = PDF − 3, PPO 8e printed folio = PDF page, PPO 9e printed = PDF − 2; Programme-Cadre NS printed = PDF − 9 (verified against the detailed programme at §5.1–§5.2); NS1 and NS2 printed = PDF page, NS3 cites PDF pages, NS4 printed = PDF − 1; the Nectar sequences cite PDF pages by séance. The placements' eleven flags are all document-side and are carried here exactly: the PCM 6e volume's maths section header mislabels its level “5ème AF” (F1 — its items are cited to the 6e volume they stand in); PCM 5e's spoken-number row slips (“six mille deux” for 6,002, and a thousands-family slip in item 352's row) are placed corrected (F2); the PCM “orthocentre” definition error stands in the PCM **7e** volume — the diff's “(AF8)” label is the diff's own slip, disproven against the live PCM 8e section, which carries the correct circumcentre definition (F3); PCM 9e's right-asymptote and strict-inequality rows are completions of points the volume leaves silent, not errors (F4); PCM 9e's opening “années diagnostiques” ((6e), 7e, 8e) reinstate the diagnostic year the 3e-cycle fascicules dropped (F5); the PCM 7e volume announces a simple-interest objective that does not exist anywhere in its grids — nothing is placed for it, and simple interest is taught by PCM 9e and PPO 9e (F6); the Nectar “Relations entre les angles” sequence states a false corresponding-angles criterion and a mise en train with no solution, and the “Calcul littéral” sequence's séance 5 example drops a factor of x — all placed as corrected (F7, F8); the record-side faults (the map's count, the download list's count, the stale textbooks.tsv) are reported, not fixed (F9); the IPN 6e worked examples carry numerical inconsistencies (objectives 5.1, 7.1, 12.2, 16.1, 19.1, 21.1, 22.1) flagged as read and NOT asserted as print errors — the volume is an OCR-read scan and some may be digit misreads (F10); and NS2's own dating (2015 in the detailed programmes' shared headers, the school year 2008-2009 on its cover) is the book's own, not a discrepancy (F11). The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the PCM volumes' other-discipline sections and their week-by-week scheduling columns beyond each distinct objective; the IPN volumes' maths avant-propos, theme indexes, other-subject sections and Suggestions/Évaluation apparatus (except where an activity is itself the taught content); the PPO fascicules' shared front matter, Programme-Cadre theme tables, progression grids, bibliographies and annexes; the Nectar sequences' summative-evaluation séances and session-management apparatus (except where a séance's activity is the taught content); the Programme-Cadre's other subjects, and the Nouveau Secondaire framework chapters shared between it and the NS1/NS2 volumes (placed once, from the Programme-Cadre volume); and the verified blank and separator pages. Into this lesson the placements stock 11 items — all teacher/curriculum-evidence rows — from Book 1 (*PCM, 1ère AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 2 (*PCM, 2ème AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 3 (*PCM, 3ème AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 4 (*PCM, 4e AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 5 (*PCM, 5e AF (MENFP, 2019)*) and Book 6 (*IPN Programme détaillé, 4e année*).

**Book 1:** *Programme à Compétences Minimales (PCM), 1ère Année Fondamentale* (curriculum programme) — Haiti, Ministère de l'Éducation Nationale et de la Formation Professionnelle (MENFP), Direction de l'Enseignement Fondamental (DEF), November 2019; 1,290,125 bytes, 50 PDF pages. TEACHER/CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — the ministry programme's required content for the year, not a pupil textbook. Printed page = PDF page − 1.

TEACHER/CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — the ministry programme's required content for the year, not a pupil textbook. Printed page = PDF page − 1.

**Book 3:** *Programme à Compétences Minimales (PCM), 3ème Année Fondamentale* (curriculum programme) — Haiti, MENFP, Direction de l'Enseignement Fondamental (DEF), November 2019; 1,421,704 bytes, 61 PDF pages. TEACHER/CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — the ministry programme's required content for the year, not a pupil textbook. Printed page = PDF page − 1.

**Book 4:** *Programme à Compétences Minimales (PCM), 4e Année Fondamentale* (curriculum programme) — Haiti, MENFP, Direction de l'Enseignement Fondamental (DEF), November 2019; 1,473,514 bytes, 66 PDF pages. TEACHER/CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — the ministry programme's required content for the year, not a pupil textbook. Printed page = PDF page − 1.

**Book 5:** *Programme à Compétences Minimales (PCM), 5e Année Fondamentale* (curriculum programme) — Haiti, MENFP, Direction de l'Enseignement Fondamental (DEF), November 2019; 1,839,531 bytes, 75 PDF pages. TEACHER/CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — the ministry programme's required content for the year, not a pupil textbook. Printed page = PDF page − 1. (The volume's spoken-number row slips — "six mille deux" for 6,002 — are placed corrected; flag F2.)

**Book 6:** *Programme détaillé, Matières de base, 4e année* (detailed programme) — Haiti, Institut Pédagogique National (IPN); 17,211,692 bytes, 122 PDF pages. TEACHER/CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — the year's detailed teaching programme, not a pupil textbook. Image-only scan; the maths section was read by OCR in full. Printed page = PDF page − 9.

Haiti enters this lesson as teacher/curriculum evidence only: it is named in the countries line and in the rows above, but it is NOT added to the acceptance count above — every document in Haiti's in-hand Maths set is a teacher-side programme, framework or teacher sequence (MENFP / IPN / Nectar), not a pupil textbook, cited as the evidence kind it is, never counted as pupil teaching (the standing evidence-only precedent). Haiti's class for Maths is PARTIAL (evidence-only): every in-hand Maths document has been read in full and stocked, but no pupil Maths textbook is in hand.

## Suriname added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Suriname's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **18 books: 13 pupil Maths books, 3 teacher Maths books and 2 kindergarten curriculum books** (20 files examined; two further in-hand files, the LJ3 OJW *Leerlingenwerkboek* and *Ik-boek*, were checked and excluded — drawing and orientation activities with no rekenen strand and no number teaching) — and placed **376 items: 344 pupil teaching items, 7 teacher-evidence items and 25 curriculum-evidence items**. No Suriname row existed in any maths lesson before this merge, so every placed item is new here. **Set verdict, proved from the books themselves:** the expansion map's count is right for the Mathematics-labelled set (13 pupil + 3 teacher = 16 files), but its kind labels are wrong in three places: (1) the LJ6 “Leerkrachtenhandleiding Deel 1” of the records is the programme's **Algemene Inleiding** volume (Book 14), not a lesson-by-lesson guide to Deel 1; (2) the two LJ7 teacher books are **oplossingenboeken — answer keys** (Books 15–16), teacher evidence; (3) Book 4 is a **rekenboek** (lesson book), not the “Leerlingenboek” textbooks.tsv names it — the same book. **Maths was hiding under another subject label once:** the KG *Spelend leren* Themaboek 3 and 4 (Books 17–18), filed as “Play-based learning” with textbooks.tsv kind “student” — also wrong: they are **teacher curriculum books** — each carry twelve *voorbereidend rekenen* (preparatory maths) sections with stated learning goals; their 24 items are stocked as **curriculum evidence**, never pupil teaching. **Partial volumes, named as such:** Books 4 and 5 are **Deel A only** and Book 12 is **Deel 1 only**; the download list's remark that LJ8 Deel 2 is “already held” is **not borne out by any record in hand** (textbooks.tsv, the staging books.json and the push-state carry only Deel 1 for LJ8) — LJ8 Deel 2 is treated as not in hand, and the LJ4/LJ5 B-volumes and LJ8 Deel 2–3 are link-only or unrecorded, cited nowhere. Book 8 (LJ6 Deel 3) is staged and pushed but has no textbooks.tsv row — the map counted it, the TSV does not carry it. **The six flagged claims are carried exactly:** (F1) **Book 11 (LJ7 Deel 3) has no Les 24** — contents and body both jump from Les 23 (tangram) to Les 25 (tekenen op schaal); the book holds 38 lessons, not 39, and the rows cite the 38 that exist; (F2) the LJ8-Deel-2 “already held” claim, above — contradicted, not in hand; (F3) **book error, Book 6 Les 6 (printed p. 23):** the information block adds a quarter hour to 15.45 and gives the result as “06:00” — the correct result is **16:00**, and the row teaches the corrected form; (F4) **book error, Book 7 Les 14 (printed p. 54):** the information states the area of a rectangle with a garbled formula line (the perimeter formula 2l + 2b is given correctly, and the area is worked by counting in the same lesson) — the row teaches perimeter = 2l + 2b and area by counting/calculation, the forms the lesson's own worked examples use; (F5) **book error, Book 6 Les 30 (printed p. 93):** the split-subtraction check line prints minus signs where the method needs the parts added (7,986 − 5,234 split as 2,000 + 700 + 50 + 2 = 2,752) — the method itself is taught correctly in Les 21 and the rows follow the correct form; (F6) **Book 13's kind:** textbooks.tsv files the LJ10 Reader as a pupil book; its foreword states it is a guide “voor de reken-leerkracht en leerling” (for teacher and pupil) — its items are placed as teaching content, worded as a **programme reader**, not a pupil textbook. **Text-layer caveat (not a claim):** Book 3's text layer drops the digit “1” in several headings (“splitsingen van 0” for the splitting of 10); the rows use the true values, read from the sums and context. **Checked and NOT flagged:** the answer keys (Books 15–16) match their pupil books lesson for lesson (39 and 38 lessons respectively), and Book 14's contents listing of the kwartaal-1 lessons matches Book 6. Byte sizes checkable against the gov.sr records matched exactly (Books 7, 8, 10, 11). Printed-page basis: printed page = PDF page in every book except Books 17–18 (printed = PDF − 5). The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the *Vooruit* books' week-closing revision pages (*weekafsluitingen*); Books 1–3's *herhaling* revision worksheets, *oefentoets* practice tests, *knipbladen* cut-out sheets and automatisation games, except where a sheet first teaches a point (those are placed); the *Plezier* books' closing games; Book 13's ch. 1 programme tables beyond item R1; and Books 17–18's non-maths theme sections. The *Plezier in rekenen* companion volumes found by the 2026-11-10 gov.sr census (LJ4 B leerlingenboek, LJ4 werkboek/takenboek, LJ5 takenboek/bijwerkboek) are link-only finds, not in hand — not read, not counted. Suriname counts in a lesson's taught-in figure only on the strength of its pupil rows; its teacher rows and curriculum rows are evidence — cited as the evidence kind they are and never the basis of the count (the standing Ghana evidence precedent). Into this lesson the placements stock 31 items (21 pupil, 2 teacher evidence, 8 curriculum evidence) from Book 1 (*Plezier in rekenen, Leerlingenwerkboek 1 (LJ3)*), Book 2 (*Plezier in rekenen, Leerlingenwerkboek 2 (LJ3)*), Book 3 (*Plezier in rekenen, Leerlingenwerkboek 3 (LJ3)*), Book 4 (*Plezier in rekenen, rekenboek A (LJ4)*), Book 5 (*Plezier in rekenen, lessenboek 5a (LJ5)*), Book 6 (*Vooruit met Rekenen, Deel 1 (LJ6)*), Book 9 (*Vooruit met Rekenen, Deel 1 (LJ7)*), Book 12 (*Vooruit met Rekenen, Deel 1 (LJ8)*), Book 14 (*Vooruit met Rekenen — Algemene Inleiding (LJ6)*), Book 17 (*Spelend leren, Themaboek 3 (KG)*) and Book 18 (*Spelend leren, Themaboek 4 (KG)*).

**Book 1:** *Plezier in rekenen — Leerlingenwerkboek 1, Leerjaar 3* (pupil workbook) — Suriname, Ministerie van Onderwijs (MinOV), eerste herziene druk 2018; 15,154,517 bytes, 56 PDF pp. Pupil workbook. Printed page = PDF page.

**Book 2:** *Plezier in rekenen — Leerlingenwerkboek 2, Leerjaar 3* (pupil workbook) — Suriname, MinOV, 2018; 7,048,848 bytes, 72 PDF pp. Pupil workbook. Printed page = PDF page.

**Book 3:** *Plezier in rekenen — Leerlingenwerkboek 3, Leerjaar 3* (pupil workbook) — Suriname, MinOV, 2018; 4,766,969 bytes, 60 PDF pp. Pupil workbook. Printed page = PDF page. (Text-layer caveat: this file's text layer drops the digit “1” in several headings — “splitsingen van 0” is the splitting of 10; the rows cite the true values, read from the sums and context.)

**Book 4:** *Plezier in rekenen — rekenboek A, Leerjaar 4* (pupil book) — Suriname, Minov, 2013 (the Surinamese version of *De Wereld in Getallen*); 17,741,213 bytes, 69 PDF pp. Pupil book. Printed page = PDF page. **Deel A only** — no Deel B in hand. (textbooks.tsv names it “Leerlingenboek”; it is a lesson book (rekenboek) — the same book.)

**Book 5:** *Plezier in rekenen — lessenboek 5a, Leerjaar 5* (pupil book) — Suriname, Minov, 2014; 10,033,668 bytes, 67 PDF pp. Pupil book. Printed page = PDF page. **Deel A only** — no Deel B in hand.

**Book 6:** *Vooruit met Rekenen — Deel 1, leerlingenboek, Leerjaar 6* (pupil textbook) — Suriname, Ministerie van Onderwijs, Wetenschap en Cultuur (MinOWC), 2023 (made with VUB); 4,251,212 bytes, 96 PDF pp. Pupil textbook. Printed page = PDF page. Kwartaal 1, lessons 1–30.

**Book 9:** *Vooruit met Rekenen — Deel 1, leerlingenboek, Leerjaar 7* (pupil textbook) — Suriname, MinOWC, 2022; 10,291,128 bytes, 96 PDF pp. Pupil textbook. Printed page = PDF page. Kwartaal 1, lessons 1–30.

**Book 12:** *Vooruit met Rekenen — Deel 1, leerlingenboek, Leerjaar 8* (pupil textbook) — Suriname, MinOWC, 2022; 27,880,807 bytes, 128 PDF pp. Pupil textbook. Printed page = PDF page. Kwartaal 1, lessons 1–30. **Only Deel 1 in hand for LJ8** — the download list's remark that LJ8 Deel 2 is “already held” is contradicted by every record (textbooks.tsv, the staging books.json and the push-state carry Deel 1 only); Deel 2 is treated as not in hand.

**Book 14:** *Vooruit met Rekenen — Leerkrachtenboek, Algemene Inleiding, Leerjaar 6* (teacher book) — Suriname, MinOWC, 2023; 1,978,741 bytes, 176 PDF pp. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — the programme's general introduction volume (rationale, learning lines, year planning, lesson organisation) plus the kwartaal-1 teacher material; it teaches no pupil content itself. Printed page = PDF page. (Kind correction: textbooks.tsv's “Leerkrachtenhandleiding Deel 1” is not a lesson-by-lesson guide to Deel 1 — this is the Algemene Inleiding volume.)

**Book 17:** *Spelend leren — Themaboek 3* (teacher curriculum book, for 5-jarigen) — Suriname, Ministerie van Onderwijs (MinOV); 39,964,623 bytes, 226 PDF pp. CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — a practical curriculum for kindergarten teachers; the book teaches teachers, not pupils (textbooks.tsv's kind “student” is wrong for it). Printed page = PDF page − 5. Themes 1–6, each with two *voorbereidend rekenen* (preparatory maths) sections.

**Book 18:** *Spelend leren — Themaboek 4* (teacher curriculum book, for 5-jarigen) — Suriname, MinOV; 25,089,639 bytes, 231 PDF pp. CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — as Book 17. Printed page = PDF page − 5. Themes 7–12, same structure.

## Papua New Guinea added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Papua New Guinea’s whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **32 files = 28 works, all Papua New Guinea Department of Education (NDoE), “Issued free to schools by the Department of Education”: 8 pupil textbook files (7 works — the JICA/QUIS-ME National Mathematics Textbooks, Grades 1–6, with Grades 3 and 5 in two parts each) and 24 teacher files (21 works — 9 JICA teacher’s manual files, 6 works with Grades 3–5 in two parts each, and 15 Standards Based Curriculum (SBC) teacher guides, Elementary Prep to Grade 12, with separate Advanced and General guides at Grades 11 and 12)** — and placed 503 items (102 from the pupil textbooks, 401 teacher-evidence items from the manuals and guides). The expansion map is wrong on both of its numbers (flag 1 — record-side, reported, not fixed by this merge): it said 6 student + 22 teacher in hand; the true set is 8 student + 24 teacher. The map missed three files that exist only as textbooks.tsv rows (the Grade 4 and Grade 6 full-year JICA pupil textbooks and the Grade 6 JICA manual) and did not know the Grade 2 SBC guide, which has a staging books.json row but no textbooks.tsv row and no push-state entry (flag 4). **Filing error (flag 2 — record-side): the git-tree copies recorded for the Grade 1 and Grade 2 JICA manuals are a different country’s book** — both TSV tree paths hold the identical file, a Mozambique Grade 1 teacher manual in Portuguese (“Manual do Professor, 1.ª classe”, 5,891,019 B / 216 pp, the same MD5 at both paths — the only byte-identical duplicate pair found in this hunt, and not a PNG book), which also sits in the release `mid-size-books-8` correctly named as a Mozambique book. The genuine PNG manuals were read from the GitHub release `full-quality-oversized-books`, whose assets match the staged sizes and checklist MD5s exactly; the push-state’s per-folder landing claims for those two files should be treated as unreliable until the tree paths are re-filed. Flag 3: the two manuals’ recorded education.gov.pg source URLs do not serve the staged pressings — both returned an HTML page during the read, and a later re-probe served a different pressing of the Grade 1 manual — so the staged pressings cannot be re-grabbed from the recorded source URLs. Flag 5: the Grade 9 SBC guide’s textbooks.tsv tree path 404s; the file was recovered by searching all release assets and matches books.json on size and pages. No Maths hides under other subject labels in the PNG records — all 156 staging books.json entries and all 163 textbooks.tsv rows were checked by subject, title and path. Link-only Maths not in hand and not read (it does not count against closure): the FODE distance course-books for Grades 7–12 Mathematics / Maths A / Maths G (unit PDFs) and the two OBE upper-secondary guides found 2026-11-10 (Advanced Mathematics TG, ISBN 978-9980-9924-8-2; General Mathematics TG). Book errors found on the read are taught corrected: the Grade 1 Textbook and its manual print “Subtracation” in the Chapter/Unit 12 title; the SBC guides print “Angels” for Angles in places, “Algebrain” for Algebraic (a Grade 11 guide’s Factorisation unit header) and “Orobability” for Probability (the Grade 9 guide’s strand table); the Grade 1 SBC guide’s Term-1 record prints “tenths” where tens is meant. The Grade 3 SBC programme has no Money topic where the JICA Grade 3 textbook carries one — a programme difference, not an error. Printed-page basis: JICA textbook pages cite the book’s own printed folio / contents page; JICA manuals cite the manual’s own printed contents page; SBC guides cite the guide’s printed programme/contents pages (the basis is stated in each book entry below); where a contents extraction was partly two-column-interleaved, the PDF page is given as well and marked “PDF p.”. Counting grain: one item per chapter (pupil textbooks), per unit (teacher’s manuals — the manual unit mirrors the textbook chapter of the same number and is counted once, as teacher evidence), and per named topic/unit in the SBC guides’ own programmes. The placements’ named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the end-of-book/year “Summary” chapters and units in the JICA textbooks and manuals (review apparatus), and the SBC guides’ front sections, assessment-record templates, glossaries/resources and integrated cross-subject project sections. Pupil-textbook rows are COUNTED normally; every manual and SBC guide row is teacher evidence — named in the countries line and in the rows, but outside the acceptance count. The placements flag this lesson’s Grade 1 Textbook item 1 and Prep guide item 154 “counting, reconcile with repair”: both are stocked in the table below at the counting lesson’s opening rung — numbers to 10 taught in the pupil book, and the Prep guide’s counting-to-120 assessment statement as teacher evidence — reconciled with the repaired counting lesson (the 2026-11-08 whole-arc fix) and its sub-lesson ladder. Into this lesson the placements stock 6 items — 2 pupil-textbook rows and 4 teacher-evidence rows — from Book S1 (*National Mathematics Textbook Grade 1*), Book T1 (*Mathematics Teacher's Manual Grade 1*), Book T10 (*Mathematics Teacher Guide, Elementary Prep (SBC)*), Book T11 (*Mathematics Teacher Guide, Elementary One (SBC)*).

**Book S1:** *National Mathematics Textbook Grade 1* (pupil textbook) — Papua New Guinea, Department of Education (NDoE), developed with JICA (QUIS-ME), 2024; 33,202,520 bytes, 184 PDF pp. PUPIL TEXTBOOK — counted. Printed folio = the book’s contents page (Chapter 1, printed p. 8, is PDF p. 16; printed = PDF − 8 in the body).

**Book T1:** *Mathematics Teacher’s Manual Grade 1* (teacher’s manual) — Papua New Guinea, Department of Education (NDoE), developed with JICA (QUIS-ME); 152,741,463 bytes, 344 PDF pp. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — the manual reproduces the pupil pages with lesson flow, objectives and assessment for the same content. Its 17 units mirror the Grade 1 Textbook’s chapters 1–17; manual printed pages are the manual’s own contents pages. Read from the GitHub release `full-quality-oversized-books` copy, whose size and MD5 match the staged checklist exactly: the textbooks.tsv git-tree path recorded for this manual holds a Mozambique Grade 1 teacher manual instead (flag 2), and the manual’s recorded education.gov.pg source URL does not serve this staged pressing (flag 3).

**Book T10:** *Mathematics Teacher Guide, Elementary Prep* (SBC teacher guide) — Papua New Guinea, Department of Education (NDoE), Standards Based Curriculum, 2015; 6,660,506 bytes, 206 PDF pp. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — scripted daily lesson plans (printed pp. 23–184); the topics stocked here are the guide’s own, from its Planning and Programming tables (printed pp. 8–22) and its Assessment strand tables (printed pp. 185–187). No SBC pupil textbook is in hand at any grade.

**Book T11:** *Mathematics Teacher Guide, Elementary One* (SBC teacher guide) — Papua New Guinea, Department of Education (NDoE), Standards Based Curriculum, 2015; 8,102,294 bytes, 201 PDF pp. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — scripted daily lesson plans; the topic list is the guide’s own, from its Assessment plan (printed pp. 182–183) and term assessment records. Book error noted on the read: the Term-1 record prints “tenths” where tens is meant in the place-value breakdown of 24.

## Sweden added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Sweden's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **13 files, 594 PDF pages**, of which **10 carry mathematics**: the national curriculum Lgr22, 2 SPSM pupil materials, and 7 SPSM teacher guides, notes and support publications. Sweden has **no state pupil textbook series** — textbook approval was abolished in 1991, Skolverket sets the curriculum (Lgr22), and the state producer SPSM (Specialpedagogiska skolmyndigheten) publishes special-needs materials — so the pupil teaching in hand is SPSM material for blind and deaf pupils, and nothing else: 13 pupil items (the Abakus exercise book and the Växelspelet money game), 81 teacher-evidence items and 79 curriculum-evidence items from Lgr22's Matematik kursplan, 173 items in all. The other 3 files in the read set prove on reading to carry no maths at all and are stocked nowhere: *Skrivmaterial för elever som behöver förstärkta linjer och rutor* is blank ruled/grid stationery (copying masters), the *ABC Skriva-Läsa* teacher guide is literacy only, and a generically named *Arbetsblad* worksheet (pharmacy vocabulary) was pulled in as a hidden-maths spot check and cleared negative. The expansion map is wrong on both axes (flag F4): it listed 8 files as 3 student + 5 teacher, but two of its eight are not Maths at all (F1: the Skriva-Läsa guide, filed in books.json under “Matematik; Svenska”, is a literacy guide — its only number-adjacent moment, drawing a numbered slip 2–5 to pick that many pictures (printed p. 6), is classroom apparatus; F3: the Skrivmaterial, counted as a student Maths book, is blank stationery), a third is the wrong kind (F2: *Räkna med mig*, filed as a student book, is a 105-page handbook for teachers of braille-using pupils — all 35 of its items are teacher evidence), and four maths-bearing files were missed by the map entirely — Lgr22 itself (its section 5.5 is the full Matematik syllabus), the *Arbeta med språkstörning i matematikundervisningen* support publication (filed under generic special education), *Växelspelet* (filed under “Verklighetsuppfattning”) and the *Jag handlar* teacher guide (filed under home-economics subjects). Flags F1–F4 are record/map-side: they are reported here and carried in this section; the records themselves are not edited — merges never edit the records, and no maths/books/sweden.md filing exists. The books' own errors are carried corrected: F5 — both Abakus volumes misprint their print ISBNs with a “987-” prefix (Övningsbok “987-91-28-11602-2”, Handledning “987-91-28-11600-8”; each book's PDF ISBN carries the correct 978- prefix); F6 — *Räkna med mig*'s ISBN is printed malformed as “98-91-28-10386-2”; F7 — the Abakus Handledning's division chapter (printed p. 45) says the pupil must master “multiplikation” where the sense requires division, taught corrected on the division row it touches; F8 — a box in *Räkna med mig* (printed p. 38) misnames the Abakus handbook as “Räkna med mig – handledning”. The placements' record items are stocked nowhere: R1 (Lgr22's assessment criteria, printed pp. 60–63 — they restate the central content as standards and add no content beyond the items), R2 (the Abakus Handledning's exercise sets and maths-card copying masters), R3 (the Arbeta publication's overview material), R4 (*Räkna med mig*'s apparatus), R5 (the Diamanterna card game itself is a physical product, not a book, and is not in hand — only its one-page SPSM note is) and R6 (the *Jag handlar* pupil book is recorded only as a sample that was never staged as a secured file; only its teacher guide is in hand). One basis note: the Övningsbok's multiplication and division exercise pages are abacus diagrams with no printed numerals, so those two placements rest on the book's contents page, section headings and introduction, exactly as the placements state. Sweden's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-heavy)**: every in-hand Maths-bearing file has been read and stocked, but 160 of its 173 items are teacher or curriculum evidence and its pupil side is special-needs material only. Into this lesson the placements stock 11 items: 4 curriculum evidence, 7 teacher evidence. No Sweden pupil item stocks into this lesson, so Sweden is added to the countries line above and named in the rows below as evidence only, and is NOT added to the acceptance count, on the standing evidence-only precedent.

**Book 1:** *Läroplan för grundskolan, förskoleklassen och fritidshemmet 2022* (Lgr22) (national curriculum) — Sweden, Skolverket, 2024; 1,317,913 bytes, 272 PDF pp., MD5 ea52068ef1b63a71f80885ddc7abdf95. Read for its Matematik kursplan in full (section 5.5, printed pp. 54–63) plus the förskoleklass chapter's maths content; the book's other 18 subject syllabi are out of scope for a Maths read. CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — Lgr22 states what must be taught and teaches nothing itself; its rows are worded as the requirements they are and are counted in no lesson's taught-in figure. Its assessment criteria (printed pp. 60–63) restate the central content as standards and are record item R1, stocked nowhere.

**Book 5:** *Lärarhandledning till 123-Räkna* (teacher guide) — Sweden, SPSM; 2,096,260 bytes, 17 PDF pp. (printed pp. 1–16), MD5 283165891421e043d9bf7bf74c05a94f. Guide to *123-Räkna*, SPSM's sign-language digital läromedel for deaf pupils, förskoleklass–åk 3 (special school to åk 4), built on Skolverket's “Hitta matematiken” mapping material and the national assessment support for number sense in åk 1–3; the guide quotes the Lspec11 maths syllabus (printed pp. 10–11). TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

**Book 7:** *Handledning – Diamanterna* (product/teaching note) — Sweden, SPSM, 2018, art. 10334; 144,314 bytes, 1 PDF p., MD5 14308edbe424b85de95e2442a98dcbf1. A one-page note for a tactile card game (28 cards: digits 0–10, the signs + − =, in braille and large print), placed at the grain the note itself states. The game itself is a physical product, not a book, and is not in hand (record item R5). TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

**Book 10:** *Räkna med mig* (teacher handbook) — Sweden, SPSM, 2018, best.nr 10386; 20,465,920 bytes, 105 PDF pp., MD5 5fbeac506f239001f466a59aa7574d9e. A 105-page handbook on mathematics teaching for compulsory-school pupils who use braille, addressed to teachers (foreword, printed p. 7) and built on the Lgr11 central content; grabbed from its recorded source_url after the GitHub Contents API returned 404. Filed in the records as a student book — wrong (flag F2): it is not a pupil book, and all 35 of its items are teacher evidence. Book errors: its ISBN is printed malformed as “98-91-28-10386-2” (flag F6), and a box on printed p. 38 misnames the Abakus handbook as “Räkna med mig – handledning” (flag F8); the Abakus guide placed from Book 4 is the real book. Its apparatus — the accessible-learning-environment and adapted/national tests chapters (printed pp. 14–26), the tools and materials catalogue (pp. 85–91), the maths word list (p. 95), the table of common signs in maths braille (pp. 96–97) and the copying masters (pp. 98–103) — is record item R4, stocked nowhere. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY.

## Switzerland added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Switzerland's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **8 files / 6 works, and no pupil textbook among them**: the three PER brochures *Mathématiques et Sciences de la nature / Sciences humaines et sociales*, Cycles 1, 2 and 3 (CIIP, Version 3.0, août 2024), whose Mathématiques parts are placed here; the Ticino *Piano di studio della scuola dell'obbligo ticinese* (DECS, edizione perfezionata 2022), whose Matematica chapter is placed here; the *Lehrplan 21 Graubünden* (Fassung vom 15.03.2016), whose Mathematik plan is placed here — one work in three filed language editions (German, Romansh, Italian), read in full in the German edition, verified identical in structure and pagination in the other two, and cited once; and one teacher guide, *MaMa — Linee guida del progetto MaMa: Ambito Numeri e calcolo* (DECS/CeRDD, 2021), whose didactic rulings are placed here as teacher evidence — 425 items in all (364 curriculum evidence + 61 teacher-guide evidence, 0 pupil). The expansion map's kind split is WRONG (record-level; the records themselves are not edited — merges never edit the records): it recorded "3 student + 1 teacher", but the three "student" files are the PER **curriculum** brochures mislabelled — **Switzerland has 0 pupil Maths textbooks in hand**, and its prescribed pupil series (Schweizer Zahlenbuch, Mathematik Primarstufe / Sekundarstufe I (LMVZ), Mathwelt, Mathbuch, MER Mathématiques 1–11, Ticino's Base Matematica 1–4) are commercial and licence-gated, not in hand and not read. Further maths was found hiding under "all subjects" labels: the Ticino Piano and the Lehrplan 21 Graubünden are all-subjects volumes carrying full mathematics chapters. Every grabbed file was verified SHA-256 byte-exact against its books.json record. Checked in hand and excluded with reasons, stocked nowhere: the three PER *Aperçus des contenus* (one example-grain maths page each — record items R1–R3), the PER *Présentation générale* (framework structure only), the PER *Éducation numérique* brochure (the computer-science curriculum; mathematics appears only as cross-links — a separate school subject) and the four Vaud *<DÉ>CODAGE* digital-education teacher manuals (contents pages of all four read: Science informatique / Médias / Usages only — a separate school subject); inside the PER brochures, the Sciences de la nature and Sciences humaines et sociales parts are other subjects, the *Modélisation* objectives are process apparatus whose content is carried by the per-theme problem-solving items, and the Lexiques are apparatus; inside the Ticino volume, every chapter other than Matematica is another subject and the process/attitude competence targets carry no subject content; inside the Lehrplan 21 volumes, every subject other than Mathematik is out of scope and the Einführung/Gesamtüberblick chapters are structure, not content; MaMa §§1 and 3 are project apparatus, and §2's quoted Ticino targets are placed at the Ticino items, not double-counted (record item R4). One within-document inconsistency is flagged so it is not stocked: the PER Cycle 1 *Aperçus des contenus* claims Cycle 1 pupils "calculate lengths (perimeter of a rectangle), areas, volumes (cube)" — the governing Cycle 1 brochure requires only comparison and measurement with an étalon (items 46–55), and perimeter/area/volume calculation begins in Cycle 2 (items 158–160); the brochure governs, the Aperçu's Cycle 1 sentence is an overstatement, and it is NOT stocked as a Cycle 1 requirement (record item R1). One document error is called out by a source itself and taught corrected: the MaMa guide rules that there is no "dissociative property" of addition — splitting an addend and associating (16 + 19 = 16 + 10 + 9) is the associative property, and textbooks that name it otherwise are wrong (item 378); the guide's other flagged classroom errors are misconceptions it warns against, not errors it commits. No Liechtenstein file is judged here: Liechtenstein teaches from Switzerland's books, but no file is filed under Liechtenstein in any record. Printed-page basis: PER brochures, Ticino Piano and Lehrplan 21 — printed folio = PDF page − 2; MaMa — printed page = PDF page. Switzerland's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-only)**: every in-hand Maths-bearing file has been read and stocked, but all 425 of its items are curriculum or teacher-guide evidence and no pupil textbook is in hand. Into this lesson the placements stock 33 items (27 curriculum evidence and 6 teacher evidence) from Book 1, Book 2, Book 3, Book 4, Book 5 and Book 6. Switzerland is added to the countries line and named in the rows below as evidence only, and is NOT added to the acceptance count, on the standing evidence-only precedent.

Switzerland's class for Maths is PARTIAL (evidence-only): the whole in-hand set is read and stocked, and it advances only when a Swiss pupil Maths textbook — one of the prescribed commercial series, today licence-gated — lands in hand and is read.

## Timor-Leste added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Timor-Leste's whole in-hand library from the PDFs — **5 files**, of which **2 carry mathematics**: the Grade 5 pupil exercise book *Livru Ezersísiu Matemátika 5* and the Matemátika section (printed pp. 318–511) of *Livru Planu Lisaun Klase 3 Períodu 2*, the combined all-subject Grade 3 Period-2 lesson-plan volume. The other three files were checked in full and carry no maths teaching: *Livru Dalan Tetun ba Klase 5* (a Tetum grammar book — number words appear once, as one of eight grammatical word-classes), *Livru Planu Lisaun Klase 6 Períodu 2* (Natural and Social Science plans only; its four “matemátika” mentions sit inside one density lesson) and *Um Mundo de Histórias 6º ano* (a Portuguese reader; maths appears only inside stories). The expansion map's “1 student + 0 teacher” split counts only the books filed as Maths and repeats a filing error — **flag F1** (record-side: reported here, not fixed; merges never edit the records, and no `maths/books/timor-leste.md` filing exists): the Grade 3 volume is filed as the “Ciências Naturais Grade 3 Teacher Guide”, claiming Natural Science plans from p. 10 and Social Science plans from p. 468, but its own Índise (printed p. 3) lists all nine basic-education components and puts Matemátika at p. 318, Siénsia Naturál at p. 512 and Siénsia Sosiál at p. 624 — the mis-filing hid the country's only in-hand Maths teacher evidence. **Flag F2** (record gap): `textbooks.tsv` carries only 3 Timor-Leste rows — the two Grade 5 books, including the Maths exercise book, sit in `books.json`, the push state and the GitHub repo tree with no TSV row. Everything else Timor-Leste Maths is link-only and not in hand — the Matemática G1–G4 student books, the G10–G12 student manuals, the 2024 new-curriculum Grade 7 set and the Learning Passport book courses — and is cited nowhere. The placements number **177 items: 83 pupil-workbook items and 94 teacher-evidence items** (33 from full printed lesson plans; 61 from the period plan table alone, each marked “(plan table only)”). The workbook is a genuine pupil teaching book — 77 numbered exercise sets (Ezersísiu 1–77) plus two front-matter reading tables — but it practises and tests; it does not present topics first (its letter to teachers states the exercises follow the annual plan and lesson-plan sequence), and it is not the main Grade 5 textbook. The guide's Matemátika section is **Period 2 only** — the Period 1 and Period 3 volumes are not in hand, so Grade 3 coverage here is one period of three. The books' own errors are taught corrected: **E1** — the workbook's census table heads its second column “2005”, but its figures are the 2010 census and its questions ask about 2010; **E2** — Ezersísiu 70 is a reprint of Ezersísiu 68, counted once (as item 70); **E3** — Ezersísiu 60's fourth prism has equal dimensions in the Tetum text and different dimensions in the Portuguese (the Tetum governs); **E4** — the guide's Lisaun 51 plan tells the teacher to write “6 x 1 = 1” (read 6 x 1 = 6, as the page's own explanation gives); **E5** — the guide's introduction states 2 formal formative assessments per period while the plan schedules 6 (assessments are exclusions either way); **E6** — ten blank plan-table content cells are continuation/test rows, folded into their parent items, and lesson 84's body settles it as the fraction–decimal relation lesson. The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the workbook's covers, imprint, forewords, letters and “do not write in this book” notice; the guide's discipline introduction and assessment guidance (printed pp. 326–328); its 22 consolidation lessons, 6 formal-assessment lessons and 5 project lessons (no new content stated for any of them); blank continuation/test rows; homework and in-lesson revision steps; and the Grade 3 volume's other eight subject sections. Counting grain: pupil book — one item per distinct practised skill; teacher guide — one item per distinct taught content in the period plan. Timor-Leste's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — a pupil-workbook set on the Peru pattern: the workbook's rows count, the guide's rows are teacher evidence and count nowhere. Into this lesson the placements stock 8 items: 0 pupil-workbook + 8 teacher evidence. Every Timor-Leste row in this lesson is teacher evidence, so Timor-Leste does not count here.

**Book 1:** *Livru Planu Lisaun Klase 3 Períodu 2* (teacher guide — the combined all-subject Grade 3 Period-2 lesson-plan volume; its Matemátika section only) — Ministério da Educação de Timor-Leste; 15,881,272 bytes, 829 PDF pp., MD5 18b0a4e0d55af5f56868c556dc9893f2. Its Índise (printed p. 3) lists all nine basic-education components; the Matemátika section (printed pp. 318–511) was read in full — full 50-minute lesson plans (objective, materials, timed teaching steps; the MAT3.x codes are the national curriculum's Grade 3 outcomes) plus the period plan table at printed pp. 318–325. Printed page = PDF page. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — its rows are named in a lesson but counted in no taught-in figure, and the section is Period 2 only. Filed in the library records as the “Ciências Naturais Grade 3 Teacher Guide” — the filing error F1 carried in the intro above.

Timor-Leste's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** (pupil-workbook set, Peru pattern — its workbook rows count in the lessons where they stock). Flags F1 (the Grade 3 volume's mis-filing) and F2 (the missing Grade 5 `textbooks.tsv` rows) are library-record matters — reported here, not fixed by this merge.

## Samoa added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Samoa's whole in-hand Maths library from the PDFs — **9 files: 8 pupil textbooks + 1 curriculum statement, 0 teacher guides** — and the expansion map's “7 student Maths books, 0 teacher” count is **wrong**: the map missed the *Mathematics Year 12* typeset edition, staged in the records as a supposed workbook (“full-year-wk”) but proved from its title page and interior to be the complete MESC Year 12 pupil textbook (Units 1–8 + answers), and it missed the kind of the *Primary Curriculum Statement, Years 1–8*, which is a curriculum document, not a pupil book. The library's enumerated slot for the separate 113 MB scanned Year 12 edition is NOT in hand (live 404; only a truncated 1 MB archive stub exists) — that edition is cited nowhere and the slot stays a library-side matter; the complete typeset Year 12 pupil textbook in hand is the book read. No teacher guide for Maths exists in the Samoa set, and no Maths content was found hiding under another subject label. The eight pupil books are real pupil teaching books — lessons, worked examples and exercises addressed to the learner — so Samoa's pupil rows **count** in a lesson's taught-in figure. The curriculum statement (CMAD, MESC, January 2013) states what is REQUIRED to be taught in Samoa primary schools and teaches nothing itself: its rows are **curriculum evidence only** and count in no taught-in figure — Samoa has no primary pupil textbooks in hand, so every primary (Years 1–8) Samoa row in the course is curriculum evidence. Counting grain: one item per distinct teaching section of a pupil book (the book's own numbered sections and their named subsections), and one item per coded learning outcome (NR / PA / DA / MS / SG / WM codes) of the curriculum statement; worked examples and exercise sets inside a section are part of that section's item, not separate items. The books' own errors are taught corrected: Year 10 Book 2's §7.1 heading prints “Drawing an Englargement” (for Enlargement); Year 11 Book 2's unit opener prints “Dealing with Angles Larger than 900” (for 90°); Year 13's contents lists chapter 2 as “Linear Inequations” while the chapter's printed title is “Linear Equations” and it teaches both — placed under the printed title. Named exclusions, stocked nowhere: the answers sections of every pupil book; Year 13's “Problems and Investigations” end-of-chapter sections, its Glossary/Index and its formula Appendix; calculator-keystroke asides (the mathematics is placed, the keystrokes are not); the curriculum statement's strand progression tables (printed pp. 81–120), which restate its coded outcomes; and Year 12's Unit 8 project unit, placed as one item. Into this lesson the placements stock 6 items: 6 curriculum evidence.

**Book 1:** *Mathematics — Primary Curriculum Statement, Years 1–8* (curriculum statement) — CMAD, Ministry of Education, Sports and Culture (MESC), January 2013; ISBN 978-982-517-089-1; written by Prof. Karoline Afamasaga-Fuata'i; 1,601,230 bytes, 120 PDF pp., MD5 ebb1213da1d69215fd1724aaf018ed34. Title page read directly. Printed page = PDF page. CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — the statement states what is required to be taught in Samoa primary schools and teaches nothing itself; its rows are named in a lesson but counted in no taught-in figure. Its rows below cite the statement's year section (Years One–Eight, printed pp. 27–79); the strand progression tables (printed pp. 81–120) restate the coded outcomes and are not re-itemised.

## Turkmenistan added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Turkmenistan's whole filed in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **5 pupil files = 3 pupil works, plus 1 teacher guide** (6 documents, 840 PDF pages in all) — and the expansion map's "3 student + 0 teacher" kind split is **wrong** (**flag F1**: the map counts one file per grade/track slot; it misses the second parts of both Turkmen works and the teacher guide entirely — any plan sized on "3 books" under-sizes the read by about 2.5×). The pupil works are the complete Turkmen-medium Grade 1 and Grade 2 courses — *Matematika* Parts 1 and 2 for each grade (Atabaýew & Öwezowa, 3rd editions, 2019/2020) — and Part 1 only of the Russian-medium Grade 1 course (Rulieva, Chernikova, Mavlyanova & Satdykova, 4th ed. 2022), a separate work by a separate author team, not a translation of the Turkmen pair; the two Grade 1 Turkmen books are scans with no text layer and were read from page renders. The one teacher book, N. Açilowa's *Başlangyç synplarda matematikany okatmagyň usulyýeti* (Methodology of Teaching Mathematics in the Primary Classes, Ylym, 2015), is a teacher-training study guide: it teaches NO pupil content, so its rows are **teacher evidence only** and count in no lesson's taught-in figure; the five pupil books are real pupil textbooks, recommended by the Ministry of Education of Turkmenistan and served by the Ministry's own E-kitaphana, so their rows **count**. **Flag F2** (record-side: reported here, not fixed; merges never edit the records, and no `maths/books/turkmenistan.md` filing exists): a `books.json` note claims the Russian Grade 1 Part 2 "is E-kitaphana post 507" — post 507 is in fact **Grade 2 Russian Mathematics Part 1** (per the enumeration table and the grid match), no Grade 1 Russian Part 2 post exists in the catalogue, and that Part 2 is recorded nowhere as found; post 507 must not be double-counted as both a Grade 1 and a Grade 2 book, and the Russian Grade 1 work stands in hand as Part 1 only. **Flag F3**: the filing notes for these books rested on samples ("every-fifth-page verification", contents lists not yet verified); this full re-read confirms the sampled claims spot-checked — e.g. the kilogram section of Grade 1 (Turkmen) Part 2 does sit at printed pp. 63–64 — and supplies the missing contents lists for both Grade 2 parts (Part 1 contents at PDF p. 103; Part 2 at PDF pp. 118–119); no sampled content claim checked was found false. **Flag F4**: the map's "2 link-only" figure cannot be confirmed and looks far too low — the banked E-kitaphana grid match carries Maths links for the Grade 2 Russian pair and for Grades 3–9 Mathematics, Algebra and Geometry, plus a Grade 5 methodology guide; every one of those is link-only, not in hand and cited nowhere, and everything beyond Grade 2 is outside this set. Byte-identical duplicate posts are cited once, on the filed copy (post 403 = the Grade 1 Part 2 file; post 412 = the Grade 2 Part 1 file; post 418 = the Grade 2 Part 2 file; post 576 = the teacher guide's post 555 file), and every pupil file was pulled from its GitHub release asset at the exact recorded byte size (reconcile sweep #8 lists 23/23 Turkmenistan entries secured). Counting grain: one item per distinct teaching section of a book (its contents-list section, or a headed teaching block where the contents list runs broader), with consolidation/revision sections counted once per book-part; pure exercise pages inside a section are apparatus of that section's item. In every book in this set the printed folio equals the PDF page. ## Tunisia added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Tunisia's whole in-hand Maths set from the file — exactly 1 book, a teacher's guide, and no pupil textbook: the *Mathematics — Teacher's Book, Year 1 of Basic Education* (CNP code 502107P00). The expansion map is CORRECT for the in-hand set (0 student + 1 teacher), with one title refinement carried everywhere: the library/checklist title says “grades 1–2” because the checklist's one teacher-guide row spans both volumes, but this file is the **Year 1 volume only** — its cover names Year 1 alone, and the Year 2 volume is the separate file 502205P00. The guide is the only mathematics row for Tunisia in `textbooks.tsv` (75 Tunisia rows: Arabic, English, French, philosophy, Spanish and this one) and the only mathematics entry in any staging `books.json`; no Maths hides under another subject label and there is no “all subjects” Tunisia volume. The book was grabbed byte-exact through the authenticated GitHub API (9,205,715 bytes, 184 PDF pages, MD5 d017ae08ae7c35d335757b0fb1ef747f — matching the library's verified record for the complete Wayback capture of 502107P00; staging holds no Tunisia PDF on disk) and read in full from rendered pages — the PDF's text layer is glyph-encoded (extraction is mojibake) and Arabic OCR returned nothing on these watermarked scans; the diagonal CNP watermark does not hide body text. Printed folio = PDF page throughout. Counting grain: one item per distinct concept, skill or teaching point the guide itself teaches or explains, at the grain the guide presents it; lesson-plan conduct prose, activity scripts and assessment apparatus are not separate items unless they carry a distinct mathematical teaching point. Where the guide itself separates teacher background theory from what Year 1 pupils actually do, the item says so — the teacher-background items (16, 17, 28, 57, 58, 75, 76 and 77) are placed as teacher background, exactly as the placements word them. The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the life-skills chapter (printed pp. 55–67 — general life-skills/21st-century-skills pedagogy, no mathematics content); the error-remediation procedure, didactic-contract discussion and remediation planning (printed pp. 45–50 passim — teaching apparatus; the content-bearing error examples are placed as items); the model lesson plans (printed pp. 78–132) beyond the points placed from the distributed sequence; and the pupil-facing integration and problem-solving worksheets of Part Three (printed pp. 158–179), practice material enacting the placed concepts and introducing none the reference or sequence sections do not already teach. Record items R1–R5 are apparatus or exam evidence, stocked nowhere as teaching items: R1 the assessment typology — diagnostic before new learning, formative during, certifying at a period's or year's end (printed pp. 41–42); R2 the six period evaluations (printed pp. 134–152) — exam evidence only; R3 the closing Arabic–French glossary of mathematical terms (printed pp. 154–157); R4 the guide's statement (printed p. 158) that the pupil textbook is held to at most 120 pages, so the extra integration and problem-solving situations are printed in this guide instead — the pupil book and this guide are designed as one package; R5 the bibliography (printed pp. 180–182). Set boundary: the pupil textbooks for Years 1, 2 and 6 (102105P01, 102105P02, 102210P00, 102609P00), the Year 2 volume of this teacher's guide (502205P00) and all later-year najahni/exercice.tn material are exact-link only — recorded links, never downloaded, in no `books.json`, not in hand and not read. The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Trinidad and Tobago's whole in-hand Maths-bearing set from the files — **9 files, all curriculum/teacher evidence, 0 pupil textbooks**: the dedicated *Primary School Curriculum — Curriculum Guides: Mathematics (Infant 1 – Standard 5)* (MoE, Curriculum Planning and Development Division, 2013), read in full; seven per-level all-subject Primary Curriculum Guides (Infants 1, Infants 2, Standards 1–5), recorded in every record under the plain “Curriculum Guide” label, whose Mathematics sections are the **same curriculum text** as the dedicated guide's section for that level — verified file by file (normalised-text sequence similarity 0.9973 Infants 1, 0.9977 Infants 2, 0.9975 Standard 2, 0.9986 Standard 5; word-multiset containment 1.0000 Standard 1 and 0.9948 Standard 4, whose per-level productions extract in a different column order; Standard 3's scanned guide verified by OCR — rationale “What Is Mathematics About?” at PDF p. 60, “MATHEMATICS: STANDARD 3” tables at PDF pp. 70/80/90, three content spot-checks matching) — so they are **cited once, through the dedicated guide** (placements Record items R1–R7), never double-counted; and the *National Early Childhood Care and Education Curriculum Guide* (ages 3–4), whose mathematics strand is placed separately (items 245–249) because the dedicated guide starts at Infant 1. The expansion map's set picture (0 student + 1 teacher) is wrong as a set description: the dedicated guide it counted is real, but the true in-hand set is these 9 files, and **no pupil Maths textbook for Trinidad and Tobago exists in hand under any label** (all pupil textbooks are commercial — STP Caribbean Mathematics and others; none is held). Every document in the set is a Ministry curriculum guide — a document that states what is REQUIRED to be taught; it teaches nothing itself — so every Trinidad and Tobago item is a curriculum REQUIREMENT item, worded as curriculum evidence. Counting grain: one item per numbered Outcome in the guide's OUTCOMES column, with the placements' stated folds (a bare “solve problems involving X” outcome that restates its own section is folded into that section's item; per-measure vocabulary outcomes are folded into their measure's item; each strand's closing Language outcome is its own item). The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the dedicated guide's front matter (PDF pp. 1–26 — contents, National Curriculum Framework prose, Subject Rationale); the Dispositions columns of every table (attitudes and values — apparatus, never items); the per-level guides' non-Mathematics subjects; the 17 Forms 1–3 guides staged at grade-08 (ELA, ICT, Spanish, Integrated Science, Social Sciences, VAPA, Visual Arts, Dance, Drama, Music, PE, HFLE, Technology Education — checked book by book; none is a Mathematics book; the Social Sciences staging anchor was labelled “Teacher's Guide” in its source bundle, but the file itself is the Social Sciences Curriculum Guide — a records mislabel, noted, not a Maths item); and the ECCE guide's non-mathematics strands and apparatus-level mentions (§5.9.9 equipment list; §5.9.16 a number line 1–10 in a materials list). Book errors are read corrected: in Standard 4's Number Patterns table the Outcomes-column wording of Outcome 5 is displaced across Outcomes 4 and 6 in print — the requirement was read from its elaborations (the items 183/184 boundary) and is placed corrected; a Standard 4 Decimals conversion elaboration cross-cites code [1.1.5] where [1.1.15] is meant, read as 1.1.15; and fraction glyphs (halves, quarters, fifths, tenths) are dropped at several points in the dedicated guide's text layer — the fractions were read from context and the surrounding elaborations. ## Zimbabwe added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Zimbabwe's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **9 files, all teacher's guides, 0 pupil books**: the Secondary Book Press *PlusOne* (Heritage-Based Curriculum) Mathematics Teacher's Guides for ECD A, ECD B and Grades 1–7. The expansion map is CORRECT for Zimbabwe Maths (0 student + 9 teacher) — no correction. Every file was grabbed from GitHub via the authenticated API (the publisher source URLs returned a Cloudflare challenge page, not the PDFs — a route failure, recorded as placements note N2 and not used) and matches its staging `books.json` record on byte size, SHA-256 and pdfinfo page count exactly (745 PDF pages in total); title/imprint pages were read directly, and contents-against-pages completeness was checked for every book — all 9 are COMPLETE full guides, no sampler or extract among them. No Maths content hides under another subject label: the other 42 in-hand Zimbabwe files are the same PlusOne TG series in English, ChiShona, Science and Technology, Social Science and Physical Education and Arts — none is an "all subjects" volume and none carries Maths units. The ZIMSAKE learner's books once counted were proved publisher SAMPLERS and removed in 2026-11 — 8 of 8 ZIMSAKE files ever pulled are proven samplers, none is a Maths book and none is in hand; the Grade 7 Mathematics *Revision Guide* is not in hand and is an excluded class in any case (revision guides do not count). Counting grain: one item per contents sub-lesson (ECD guides, whose units are sequences of workbook-page lessons) or per numbered unit (Grades 1–7), split only where one unit plainly teaches two distinct course lessons. Pages cited are the books' printed pages (printed folio = PDF page − 4 in the ECD guides; Grades 1–7 unit starts are the contents printed pages, verified against the folios). Book errors found on the read are taught corrected and recorded: the Grade 4 contents lists Unit 17 Money at printed p. 59 and Unit 18 Time at p. 58 — the page order in the contents is reversed; the units themselves run Money (objectives printed p. 56) before Time, and the Money placement cites the objectives page; the Grade 6 Unit 23 (Time) guide cites its Learner's Book as "pages 18−204" — a dropped digit (the unit's own exercises sit at Learner's Book pp. 189–204), a citation slip in the guide that does not affect the teaching content; and the Grade 7 contents misnumbers the final Data handling unit as "Unit 21" although it follows Unit 30 — the body heads it Unit 31, and the placement records both numbers. The Grade 4 Unit 5 decimal comma (3,1 for three-and-a-tenth) is the book's consistent notation, recorded as printed, not treated as an error; decimals are written with the decimal comma throughout Grades 4–7. Record note N1: `phase3-push-state.json` still lists only 4 Zimbabwe folders (paperwork) although all 51 Zimbabwe files — including these 9 — are confirmed live on GitHub (sweep #8, 2026-11-05); the state-file gap is already on record, and this read re-confirms the 9 Maths files on GitHub at exact recorded sizes and hashes. Zimbabwe is **TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE ONLY** in every lesson: every item is worded as what the guide states the teacher teaches and what learners should be able to do, Zimbabwe is named in the countries line and in the rows below, and it is added to NO lesson's acceptance count, on the Ghana precedent. Zimbabwe's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-only)**. Into this lesson the placements stock 16 teacher-evidence item(s).

## Zambia added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Zambia's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **13 files, 626 PDF pages, and every one of them teacher evidence: the in-hand set holds no pupil textbook at all**. The expansion map is WRONG for Zambia Maths on both numbers: it listed 2 student + 7 teacher, but the true recorded split is 2 student + 11 teacher (the map's teacher count omitted the 4 KG Pre-Mathematics teacher files), and on opening BOTH files recorded as "student" proved to be teaching modules — the Grade 7 "Mathematics 2" file is the *Mathematics II Teaching Module, Form 1 Term 2* (its preface states it "is designed to help teachers prepare and deliver Competence-Based lessons"), and the KG1 "Pre-Mathematics and Science" file is the *Intellectual Disabilities Teaching Module, Term 3* (its cover is titled "TEACHING MODULE" and its interior is teacher guidance with teacher/learner role tables and a lesson-plan appendix). The two kind mis-records live in `textbooks.tsv` and are flagged in the expansion diff for the record-keepers — record-side, reported here, not fixed by this merge (merges never edit the filings or records). The set: ECE Pre-Mathematics and Science teaching modules (KG1 mainstream Terms 2 and 3, KG2 mainstream Term 3, and the Intellectual Disabilities adaptations for Terms 2 and 3); combined Mathematics and Science teaching modules for Grade 1 (Terms 2 and 3) and Grade 2 (Term 3) — only their mathematics content is placed here, the science halves are the placements' named exclusions; the Grade 5 *Numeracy Teacher's Guide* of the Catch Up Programme (MoE/VVOB, 2023), whose TaRL method — assessing each learner one-to-one and teaching learners grouped by their current level, not their grade — is Zambia's own adopted way of teaching foundational numeracy; and pure Mathematics teaching modules for Grade 4 (Term 3) and Grade 7 (Mathematics I Terms 2 and 3, Mathematics II Term 2). All 13 files were fetched fresh for the read (12 via the authenticated GitHub Contents API from the textbooks repo tree; the Grade 5 Catch Up guide via its recorded VVOB source URL at exactly the recorded byte size), verified by byte size, MD5, pdfinfo page count and cover/title page, and read in full; all 13 MD5s differ — no duplicates. Not in hand and cited nowhere: the CDC Digital Library *Mathematics and Science Teaching Module* for Form 2 (visual-impairment), which exists only as a banked lead with no file, and every Zambian pupil textbook, which sits behind a commercial wall (Longman/ZEPH/Oxford/MK) with no free file — the one further free lead is itself another teaching module, so it would not change the class even if secured. Zambia's own teaching ways are placed as content in their own right: local materials, Zambian languages, kwacha money and TaRL level grouping. Book errors found on the read are taught corrected and recorded in the placements: the Grade 7 Mathematics II module swaps the definitions of Profit and Loss in its Key Terms (printed p. 66), prints "the sum of exterior and exterior angles add up to 180°" where interior + exterior is meant (printed p. 57), mixes K600 000 with K60 000 and asks for "the price of the bag" in a laptop question in its end-of-topic assessment (printed pp. 77–78), gives a bacterium "6 300 000 meters long" (printed p. 9), and its cover and imprint-page ISBNs disagree; the Grade 7 Mathematics I Term 3 module's Pythagoras assignment sends a walker 3 km east and then 4 km **west** (printed p. 3) — as written no 3–4–5 triangle arises; the Grade 7 Mathematics I Term 2 module gives Lusaka–Kitwe as 845 km in one bullet and Kitwe–Lusaka as 357 km in another (printed pp. 15–16), prints "Complementary angles add up to 900" for 90° (printed p. 27), and carries a simplifying tip that reads as though unlike terms can be added or subtracted — only like terms combine (printed p. 6); the Grade 5 Catch Up guide leaves its own unfinished editorial query "(please check this line for meaning)" standing in the printed division lesson (printed p. 41) — the line it queries is still understandable and is placed as written; and the Grade 2 module's subtraction demonstration names the wrong character ("take 13 sticks from Samuel", PDF p. 37 — the module's characters are Bupe and Mutinta) and first asks for 13 sticks where its own word problem needs 15 (PDF p. 23). Zambia is **TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY** in every lesson: every item is worded as what the module or guide states the teacher teaches, Zambia is named in the countries line and in the rows below, and it is added to NO lesson's acceptance count, on the Ghana precedent. Into this lesson the placements stock 9 teacher-evidence item(s).

**Book 1:** *Numeracy Teacher's Guide, Catch Up Programme (Grades 3–5)* — Zambia, Ministry of Education with VVOB (compiled with Pratham, ZESSTA, TaRL Africa, UNICEF and others), 2023; 11,394,383 bytes, 52 PDF pp., MD5 4c1c78eb0f7fd45bab66bf5af99084c0. Teacher's guide — TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY; the TaRL method it scripts is Zambia's own adopted way of teaching foundational numeracy, and its activities are placed as content. Filed at Grade 5 in the library (the band's benchmark grade); the guide itself serves Grades 3–5. Fetched via its recorded VVOB source URL at exactly the recorded byte size. Printed page = PDF page − 3. Production note carried: the guide's own unfinished editorial query — "(please check this line for meaning)" — is left standing in the printed division lesson (printed p. 41); the line it queries is still understandable and is placed as written.

## Uruguay added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Uruguay's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **6 books: five pupil WORKBOOKS of the official series *Cuaderno para Hacer Matemática* (ANEP · DGEIP · CACEEM) — Inicial, Primero (5th ed. 2021), Segundo, Tercero and Quinto (5th ed. 2021) — plus one teacher book, *Matemática en el Segundo Ciclo — Libro para el Maestro* (Grades 4–6)** — and placed 340 items (304 pupil + 36 teacher-evidence). The expansion map's count is right and its kind label is wrong (flag F1 — record-side, reported here, not fixed by this merge; merges never edit the filings or the records): the five pupil books are activity **workbooks**, not student textbooks. A workbook is a pupil book (the South Africa/Peru pattern): the pupil-workbook items below are **teaching content**, so Uruguay's pupil rows are COUNTED in full. The teacher book's items are **teacher evidence only** — worded as teacher evidence, named in the countries line and in the rows, and added to no acceptance count (the Ghana precedent); the teacher book is also the only in-hand coverage of the Grade 4 and Grade 6 pupil content, whose workbooks are not in hand. **Not in hand, not read, cited nowhere**: the Grade 4 (Cuarto) pupil workbook (36,381,739 B) and the Grade 6 (Sexto) pupil workbook (50,368,322 B) — official DGEIP URLs banked, but every banked Wayback capture is crawler-truncated at 5,242,880 B, the live DGEIP host does not answer from this network, and the Grade 4 book also circulates only as a login-gated Studocu copy (not pursued, per the no-login rule) — and the Primer Ciclo teacher book (Inicial–Grade 3, 5,703,330 B), likewise link-only (captures truncated at 1,048,576 B). Record flag F2 (record-side, reported, not fixed): `textbooks.tsv` carries no row for the Grade 3 workbook, although `phase3-push-state.json` records it as pushed and its staging folder carries a books.json — the book is in hand and was read in full. The Grade 5 workbook is an image-only PDF and was read from page renders; the other five books were read from their text layers. Book errors found on the read are taught corrected: the Grade 3 workbook's garden table (printed p. 79) prints last year's figures 40, 35 and 20 with a total of 85 — the rows sum to 95; the Grade 5 workbook's worked example (printed p. 84) decomposes 0.65 as 6/10 + 9/100, which makes 0.69 — corrected to 6/10 + 5/100 (the decimal form printed beside it, 0.65 = 0.6 + 0.05, is correct); and the Grade 5 book's printed p. 14 side tab names the wrong guide character, "Lechuza" for this book's "Yacaré" — a production error only, content unaffected. (The Grade 5 book's p. 116 "almost two and a half times more casein" line is NOT an error: it is a pupil claim the book itself asks children to judge, and it is placed as such.) The placements' named exclusions are apparatus, stocked nowhere: the workbooks' cut-out pages (recortables), the Inicial personal pages (printed pp. 8–9) and its ice-melting observation page (printed p. 78 — science, no maths content), and the Grade 5 opening questionnaire, guide-character pages and recortables. Printed-page basis: printed page = PDF page − 2 in all six books. Counting grain: one item per distinct activity / teaching point in a workbook; one item per distinct teaching guidance / content statement in the teacher book. Into this lesson the placements stock 16 items — 16 pupil-workbook rows.

**Book 1:** *Cuaderno para Hacer Matemática en Inicial* (pupil workbook) — Uruguay, ANEP · CEIP/DGEIP · CACEEM, 1st edition, ISBN 978-9974-677-76-0; 7,366,728 bytes, 99 PDF pp., MD5 b7f3dbb92271a6dd4cb89140d6d128f7. PUPIL WORKBOOK — counted: the workbook teaches each topic through activities and guided practice. Printed page = PDF page − 2. Guide character: Lobito.

**Book 2:** *Cuaderno para Hacer Matemática en Primero* (pupil workbook) — Uruguay, ANEP · CEIP/DGEIP · CACEEM, 5th edition 2021, ISBN 978-9974-677-77-7; 34,876,740 bytes, 100 PDF pp., MD5 31d86de87c95365c4050dd410a2cab7a. PUPIL WORKBOOK — counted. Printed page = PDF page − 2. Guide character: Zorrito.

**Book 3:** *Cuaderno para Hacer Matemática en Segundo* (pupil workbook) — Uruguay, ANEP · CEIP/DGEIP · CACEEM; 33,927,158 bytes, 100 PDF pp., MD5 70d2a03f218e7e8be986be29ab144591. PUPIL WORKBOOK — counted. Printed page = PDF page − 2. Guide character: Mulita.

**Book 4:** *Cuaderno para Hacer Matemática en Tercero* (pupil workbook) — Uruguay, ANEP · CEIP/DGEIP · CACEEM; 5,093,386 bytes, 100 PDF pp., MD5 84d0f4c53413f2739d9b418aeaf69066. PUPIL WORKBOOK — counted. Printed page = PDF page − 2. Guide character: Guazubirá. (Record flag F2: `textbooks.tsv` carries no row for this book although push-state records it as pushed — record-side, reported, not fixed.)

| Lesson part | Where it comes from |
|---|---|
| Dice game: roll a die and take exactly that many bottle caps from a box; the player who collects the most caps wins — counting out a set to match a dice count, then comparing totals | Uruguay — *Cuaderno para Hacer Matemática en Inicial* (pupil workbook), printed p. 10 |
| Match a pictured dice throw to the caps it collects, and complete a die face to match a pictured set of caps | Uruguay — *Cuaderno para Hacer Matemática en Inicial* (pupil workbook), printed p. 11 |
| Aiming game with five balls: win by correctly saying how many you got in — counting a small set and reporting the count | Uruguay — *Cuaderno para Hacer Matemática en Inicial* (pupil workbook), printed pp. 12–13 |
| Match each die face to the numeral card with its number (1–6), then write the numerals on blank cards and draw die faces for given numerals 3 and 5 | Uruguay — *Cuaderno para Hacer Matemática en Inicial* (pupil workbook), printed pp. 18–19 |
| Fill-the-board game: spin a spinner and place that many counters on the board | Uruguay — *Cuaderno para Hacer Matemática en Inicial* (pupil workbook), printed p. 34 |
| Count a whole box of bottle caps passed hand to hand; compare counts, discuss, and agree one count as a class | Uruguay — *Cuaderno para Hacer Matemática en Inicial* (pupil workbook), printed p. 50 |
| Count the bicycles in a picture and colour the numeral track number that matches the count | Uruguay — *Cuaderno para Hacer Matemática en Inicial* (pupil workbook), printed p. 51 |
| Card game "chasing numbers" with cards 1–30: play the card one more than the card on the table; a red-square card reverses to one less — successor and predecessor | Uruguay — *Cuaderno para Hacer Matemática en Inicial* (pupil workbook), printed pp. 52–53 |
| Lottery with balls 1–30 and bingo cards: hear a number called without seeing it, find it on your card; from the balls drawn, decide which pair completed a row | Uruguay — *Cuaderno para Hacer Matemática en Inicial* (pupil workbook), printed pp. 54–55 |
| Debate: after thirty-nine, does "thirty-ten" follow? How can you check how the count continues | Uruguay — *Cuaderno para Hacer Matemática en Inicial* (pupil workbook), printed p. 71 |
| Number-strip game 1–30: guess four covered numbers; check a claim about which number is covered, and find the number just before 20 and just after 21 | Uruguay — *Cuaderno para Hacer Matemática en Inicial* (pupil workbook), printed pp. 72–73 |
| Count real sets: how many boys in the class, how many new classmates, how many pages in the reading book; count the supplies Zorrito puts in each box | Uruguay — *Cuaderno para Hacer Matemática en Primero* (pupil workbook), printed pp. 7–8 |
| Card staircases: deal Spanish cards and play one less than a turned card, building four descending stairs from the kings; complete a pictured staircase — predecessor in the count | Uruguay — *Cuaderno para Hacer Matemática en Primero* (pupil workbook), printed p. 8 |
| Jumps game: from a start card (any number 1–819) write seven numbers advancing or retreating by 10s or by 5s, checked with the calculator | Uruguay — *Cuaderno para Hacer Matemática en Segundo* (pupil workbook), printed p. 48 |
| Jumps game with start cards 500–1,000 and jump cards ±10, ±25, ±50: write seven-number series and verify with the calculator; Guazubirá's strategy for +50 from 546 (watch the tens digit and count 50, 60, 70…) | Uruguay — *Cuaderno para Hacer Matemática en Tercero* (pupil workbook), printed pp. 8–9 |
| Fun-run numbers: Guazubirá is 3,409 — write the next number and the two before; the next five repair tickets after 3,999 (crossing the thousand); race times in seconds 600, 900, 780, 540 — who won | Uruguay — *Cuaderno para Hacer Matemática en Tercero* (pupil workbook), printed p. 46 |

Uruguay is added to the countries line and to the acceptance count above on its pupil-workbook rows — every Uruguay row in this lesson is a pupil-workbook row, so Uruguay counts in full. Uruguay's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED for the in-hand set**: all 6 in-hand books have been read in full and placed; the wider set stays open only on the Grade 4 and Grade 6 pupil workbooks, which are link-only, so those two grades' pupil content rests on the Segundo Ciclo teacher book (Book 6) until a complete capture of either workbook lands.
